Campaign, seed 737, difficulty Captain, permadeath on, scheme TwoRollAverage, 9 maps
Autosaved as auto-1; the last 3 camps are kept
-- Starting Alone --
The appointment came with a seal, a coat, and a list. The list is the
company: ten names from three regions, owed to Ironwake under the
armistice. None of them has arrived. I read it every morning anyway. So
far it is the only part of the job I have done.

The keep is two days east. Someone has followed me since the ford, and
the waystation ahead has a fort with its gate still on. A fort keeps the
weather off. It does not keep anyone from shooting at it.

What the crown taught me before it sent me, which was not a lot: read
the blow before you swing it. Know who answers back and who cannot. A
sword answers a sword. It does not answer a bow.

(forecast <unit> <target> prints a strike and its counter before you
take it. threat <unit> prints who can reach you next phase. recall
rewinds to a player turn you name; you have three.)

-- Before map 1 of 9: Starting Alone; the purse holds 500 --

  Alder Fenn (captain): Cadet L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3; promotes at 3: Marshal, Ranger or Vanguard

Deploys to Starting Alone: Alder Fenn (deploy 1 of 10)
== Keep ==
Purse: 500
Rooms: beds: 10/12; a fallen member keeps their bed
  bunk: Bunk room, 400, +2 beds, built 0 of 2
  forge: Forge, 600, Refine +5 acc or +1 power a step, built 0 of 1; opens once the_tollgate is won
  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 2 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won; needs the Barracks first
  Stores: common 0, frozen iron 0; a step costs one and 100, shop weapons to +2 on common, the main line's signatures to +3 on frozen iron
Walls and ditches: The keep's menu opens after the raid on it (ironwake_raid, map 6) is fought

No side map is open.

Starting Alone won: rout; reward 300, the purse holds 800; nobody fell
-- After Starting Alone --
Three dead on a road nobody will remember. I have nobody to add to the
list yet, which is its own kind of report.

The waystation keeper says a Kestrow chaplain bound for Ironwake was
taken off the mill road two days back. Her name is on my list. East it
is, by way of the mill.

Autosaved as auto-1; the last 3 camps are kept
-- The Mill --
The mill road follows a stream that outlived its mill. The men who took
the chaplain lost her two nights ago, the keeper says, and she has been
holding the miller's house on the far bank since. Alone, two days. That
is longer than I have managed anything.

Her name is on my list: Maud, Kestrow, chaplain. The list does not say
whether she can fight. It says she mends things.

(This map is lost if Maud falls. She strikes at range 1 or 2 with
Radiance and heals with her Salve. A fight within 6 tiles of a sleeping
group wakes it; threat names what a stop would wake.)

-- Before map 2 of 9: The Mill; the purse holds 800 --
== Roster ==
  Alder Fenn (captain): Cadet L2, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 32/40 (repair 80), 2: Field Dressing 2/3; promotes at 3: Marshal, Ranger or Vanguard
  Wren: Cadet L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Teodor: Pikeman L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Ottilie: Bowman L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Bow 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Pell: Adept L1, EXP 0; 1: Cinder 8/8, 2: Gust 6/6

  Alder Fenn (captain): Cadet L3, EXP 93; 1: Iron Sword 31/40 (repair 90), 2: Field Dressing 2/3; promotes at 3: Marshal, Ranger or Vanguard
  Wren: Cadet L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Teodor: Pikeman L1, EXP 47; 1: Iron Lance 38/40 (repair 20), 2: Field Dressing 3/3

  Pell: Adept L1, EXP 0; 1: Cinder 8/8, 2: Gust 6/6
  Dunstan: Bulwark L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40
  Maud: Chaplain L2, EXP 51; 1: Radiance 5/5, 2: Salve 8/8

  Rook: Skyrider L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40

Deploys to The Tollgate: Alder Fenn, Wren, Teodor, Pell (deploy 4 of 11)
== Keep ==
Purse: 2070
Rooms: beds: 11/12; a fallen member keeps their bed
  bunk: Bunk room, 400, +2 beds, built 0 of 2
  forge: Forge, 600, Refine +5 acc or +1 power a step, built 0 of 1; opens once the_tollgate is won
  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 2 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won; needs the Barracks first
  Stores: common 0, frozen iron 0; a step costs one and 100, shop weapons to +2 on common, the main line's signatures to +3 on frozen iron
Walls and ditches: The keep's menu opens after the raid on it (ironwake_raid, map 6) is fought
== Quests ==
Side maps (the member and one ally you pick, not the captain; a lost side map never ends the campaign):
  maud_1: Maud's quest 1, The Lazar House (quest maud_1 <ally>); permadeath applies
== Shop ==
Shop: iron_sword 400, steel_sword 900, iron_lance 400, steel_lance 900, iron_axe 400, iron_bow 400, iron_gauntlets 400, hatchet 300, cinder 500, gust 600, salve 500, field_dressing 150; a seal for promotion costs 500

== Keep ==
Purse: 2080
Rooms: beds: 11/12; a fallen member keeps their bed
  bunk: Bunk room, 400, +2 beds, built 0 of 2
  forge: Forge, 600, Refine +5 acc or +1 power a step, built 0 of 1
  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 2 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won; needs the Barracks first
  Stores: common 0, frozen iron 0; a step costs one and 100, shop weapons to +2 on common, the main line's signatures to +3 on frozen iron
Walls and ditches: The keep's menu opens after the raid on it (ironwake_raid, map 6) is fought
